Tooltip: Health: +160; Defense: +50; Strength: +30; Crit Damage: +20%; Intelligence: +15; Gemstones: [⚔] [⚔]; Tiered Bonus: Dominus (0\ 4); Every 1.5s, melee attacks grant 1 stack of ᝐ Dominus. Lose 1 stack after 4s of not gaining a stack.; Each stack grants +0.05 Swing Range.; At 10 stacks swipe in a random direction dealing 0.5x Swipe Damage to mobs in its path.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; ❣ Requires Kuudra Basic Tier Completion.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Crimson Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set. There are 4 upgraded tiers of Crimson Armor: Hot, Burning, Fiery, and Infernal, which can all be obtained by prestiging the previous tier.
## Obtaining
Basic Tier Crimson Armor Pieces can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Paid Reward Chests]] at the end of a [[Kuudra]] fight.
Piece Drop Chances:
- Basic Tier: 4.31%
- Hot Tier: 4.2%
- Burning Tier: 4.05%
- Fiery Tier: 3.91%
- Infernal Tier: 4.08%
Higher tiers of Crimson Armor can be obtained by prestiging the previous tier at a blacksmith ( [[Malik]], [[Igor]], [[Alwin]], or [[The Hex]]), which can be done if it has 10 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] applied to it. After being prestiged, Armor pieces lose their Stars.

[Effects]
[Tiered Bonus: Dominus; Every 1.5s➜1s➜0.5s, melee attacks gain 1 stack of ᝐ Dominus.; Each ᝐ Dominus stack grants 0.1 Swing Range, 2 Ferocity and 10% Damage (additive).; At 10 stacks also swipe in a random direction hitting every enemy in the path of the swipe.; Lose 1 stack after 4s➜7s➜10s of not gaining a stack.; Pieces Required: 2+]
Upon having at least 2 pieces equipped, the Tiered Set Bonus: Dominus is unlocked. When hitting enemies with melee attacks, 1 stack is gained, with a cooldown dependent on how many pieces of Crimson Armor are being worn - 1.5s for wearing two pieces, 1s for wearing three, and 0.5s if wearing all four pieces. Each stack increases some of the player's stats by a certain amount, up to ten stacks:
- Basic Tier: 0.05 [[Swing Range]]
- Hot Tier: 0.1 [[Swing Range]]
- Burning Tier: 0.1 [[Swing Range]] and 1 [[Ferocity]]
- Fiery Tier: 0.1 [[Swing Range]] and 2 [[Ferocity]]
- Infernal Tier: 0.1 [[Swing Range]], 2 [[Ferocity]], and +10% [[Damage]]
Upon reaching 10 stacks of Dominus, the player performs a Swipe attack. The swipe's damage is usually 15-25% of their last melee hit. It will do double damage to the first enemy it hits and can trigger multiple times on the same enemy. Its damage is affected by the tier of armor and how many pieces are worn:
|Tier|2/4 Multiplier|3/4 Multiplier|4/4 Multiplier|
|-|-|-|-|
|Basic|0.5|1.0|1.5|
|Hot|0.625|1.25|1.875|
|Burning|0.75|1.5|2.25|
|Fiery|0.875|1.75|2.625|
|Infernal|1.0|2.0|3.0|
The swipe's damage is affected by [[Damage Calculation|Additive Damage Sources]], with higher % bonus decreasing the swipe's damage.

## Trivia
- Upon release, the tiered bonus Dominus ignored hit delay cooldown upon reaching 10 stacks. This allowed people with a decent CPS to accomplish ridiculously fast bosses and achieve an insane amount of DPS.
- Currently, this armor set is the best out-of-dungeons generalist melee armor set in the game at its Infernal Tier with 15 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]], which requires you to upgrade each set piece 4 times, resulting in the adding of 220 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] in total to the set. The admins have made clear that there is intention to eventually release an armor set stronger than Crimson, though it'd come with it's own combat content accordingly.
- Before the release of the revamped Dominus, swipes could also trigger on arrow hits.
- Upon release, Dominus could activate on magic hits, making it one of the best mage armor sets in the game outside dungeons.
- Crimson Armor was the second item, and currently only armor, to grant [[Swing Range]].
- [[Ferocity]] does not trigger Crimson armor Dominus stacks.
- A total of 141,005 (2,203 stacks plus 13) [[Crimson Essence]], 45 [[Heavy Pearl]], 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Kuudra Teeth]], and 44,740,000 [[Coins]] are required for one Infernal tier piece, costing .
  - A total of 1,793,505 (28,023 stacks plus 33) [[Crimson Essence]], 97 (1 stack plus 33) [[Heavy Pearl]], 460 (7 stacks plus 12) [[Kuudra Teeth]], and 139,175,000 [[Coins]] are needed from scratch for a single Infernal piece with 15 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]], costing .

The Crown of Avarice is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmets|Helmet]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Crown of Greed]] 2x [[Avaricious Chalice]] 24x [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 64x [[Griffin Feather]]|Recipe: [[Griffin Feather]] x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x8 ×3 + [[Crown of Greed]] + [[Avaricious Chalice]] x2 → Crown of Avarice|
1,000,000,000 [[Coins]] counter Crown of Avarice can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Helmet:
Crown of Avarice [[Health]]: +200 [[Defense]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Overindulgence; Mythological mobs deal 1.5x damage.; You gain 5x Coins from Griffin Burrows and Mythological mobs, but they are consumed by this helmet.; Grants +2.5 Magic Find against Mythological mobs and deal +0.015x Damage for each digit of Coins consumed.]
Crown of Avarice will consume 5x [[Coins]] gained by the [[Player]] from digging [[Griffin Burrows]] and killing [[Mythological]] mobs. The consumed coins will be tallied up, and will grant the player +2.5 [[Magic Find]] against [[Mythological]] mobs, and +0.015x [[Damage]] per digit of coins consumed, up to 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]] or 10 digits.
### Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|0–10,000 [[Coins]] on the item reached||
|100,000 [[Coins]]|HUNGRY! Your Crown of Avarice seems to want more coins.|
|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|STARVING! You can feel your Crown of Avarice's cravings getting stronger.|
|10,000,000 [[Coins]]|FAMISHED! Your Crown of Avarice starts growling at you for more coins. What have you gotten yourself into...?|
|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|RAVENOUS! Your Crown of Avarice is barking at you. If you don't keep feeding it coins, it might eat your head instead!|
|1,000,000,000 [[Coins]]|SATIATED! Your Crown of Avarice is stuffed to the brim, and appears to be in a food coma.|

## Notes
- The counter on the ability cannot go over 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]].
- Once the ability is maxed, the Crown of Avarice no longer consumes gained [[Coins]].
  - However, the 5x [[Coins]] multiplier changes to 2x.

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Glacite Golem pets.; This skin will allow you to swap between 12 crystallized golems!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; LEGENDARY COSMETIC
The Crystal Glacite Golem Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Crystal Glacite Golem Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on January 24, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Crystal Glacite Golem Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Glacite Golem Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 12 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 12 crystallized golems!

Tooltip: Gemstones: [⚔] [⚔]; Gains +4 Damage per Taming level and copies the base Combat stats of your active pet.; Grants +1%  Damage and +0.2 Magic Find on  Mythological mobs per Bestiary tier you have across all of them.;  Mythological Bestiary Tiers: 0; +0%  Damage +0 Magic Find; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Daedalus Blade is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Sword of Revelations]] 2x [[Daedalus Stick]] 48x [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x8 ×6 + [[Daedalus Stick]] ×2 + [[Sword of Revelations]] → Daedalus Blade|
## Usage
Sword:
Daedalus Blade
[Effects]
[Gains +4 Damage per Taming level and copies the base Combat (Stats) stats of your active Pet (Pets).; Grants +1% Damage and +0.2 Magic Find on Mythological mobs per Bestiary tier you have across all of them.]
Sword:
[[Daedalus Blade]]
[Effects]
[Gains +5 Damage per Taming level and copies your base Combat (Stats) stats of your active Pet (Pets).; Grants +1.5% Damage and +0.3 Magic Find on Mythological mobs per Bestiary tier you have across all of them.]
The Daedalus Blade's [[Damage]] depends on its wielder's [[Taming]] level. The weapon has 0 [[Damage]] at [[Taming|Taming 0]] and increases by 4 for every [[Taming]] level up to 240 at [[Taming|Taming LX (60)]].
The Daedalus Blade copies its wielder's active [[Pets|Pet]]'s Combat Stats onto itself. Only stats that are actually displayed in the pet's stats at the top are copied – meaning pet item stats are generally included, but abilities that do not add to stats are not.
This weapon, at a sufficiently high [[Taming]] level and with enough [[Mythological]] [[Bestiary]] tiers, is the optimal weapon against [[Mythological]] creatures.
## Upgrading
The Daedalus Blade can be upgraded into a Daedalus Blade with 8 [[Golden Fragment|Golden Fragments]], granting +1 [[Damage]] per [[Taming]] level, +0.5% [[Damage]] and +0.1 [[Magic Find]] on [[Mythological]] mobs per [[Mythological]] [[Bestiary]] tier.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Daedalus Blade]] 1x Daedalus Blade 8x [[Golden Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Golden Fragment]] ×8 + Daedalus Blade → [[Daedalus Blade]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Chimera]] [[Ultimate Enchantments|Ultimate Enchantment]] can be put on this weapon to grant up to 200% of the active pet's combat stats on the weapon.
- This is one of two weapons with no base stats, the other being [[Great Spook Sword]].
- The Daedalus Blade is often referred to as an axe in [[Changelog|Changelogs]] and [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha threads]].

Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Erudite reforge when combined with Ancestral Spade, Archaic Spade, and Deific Spade.; One of the greatest minds of his time.; Much can be learned from what Daedalus left behind.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Speed; Erudite Bonus Griffin Burrow chains always consist of 2 additional burrows.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 30.; LEGENDARY REFORGE STONE
Daedalus' Notes is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] applies the [[Reforging|Erudite]] reforge to the [[Ancestral Spade|Ancestral]], [[Archaic Spade|Archaic]], and [[Deific Spade]]. It grants [[Speed]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Daedalus Stick]] 4x [[Mythos Fragment]] 32x [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] 64x [[Griffin Feather]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Mythos Fragment]] ×4 + [[Griffin Feather]] x64 + [[Enchanted Paper]] x64 ×2 + [[Daedalus Stick]] + [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] x32 → Daedalus' Notes|
## Usage
### Reforging
Daedalus' Notes can be combined with an [[Ancestral Spade|Ancestral]], an [[Archaic Spade|Archaic]], or a [[Deific Spade]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Erudite]] reforge, to grant [[Speed]], which scales with the item's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXX (30)]]. An item that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Erudite]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Erudite Bonus, which will make the player's Griffin Burrow chains consist of 2 additional burrows.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+8 Spd ([[Speed]])|+11 Spd ([[Speed]])|+15 Spd ([[Speed]])|+20 Spd ([[Speed]])|+25 Spd ([[Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Erudite Bonus; Griffin Burrow chain always consist of 2 additional burrows.|Erudite Bonus; Griffin Burrow chain always consist of 2 additional burrows.|Erudite Bonus; Griffin Burrow chain always consist of 2 additional burrows.|Erudite Bonus; Griffin Burrow chain always consist of 2 additional burrows.|Erudite Bonus; Griffin Burrow chain always consist of 2 additional burrows.|
|Cost to apply|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Daemon Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Pity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Pity ([[Attributes]]); Your RNG Meter ([[Pity and RNG Meters]]) fills +1%–10% faster.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Daemon Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Daemon Shard has the following description:
- Its very presence warps reality around it, bending the laws of nature to its will and spreading chaos and despair wherever it treads.
## Trivia
- [[Attributes|Pity]] is the shortest [[Attributes|Attribute]] name alongside [[Attributes|Chop]] ( [[Hummingbird Shard]]) and [[Attributes|Veil]] ( [[Ghost Shard]]).

## Obtaining
The Dante's Uprising Greenhouse Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on April 25, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 4,000 purchases.

The Dark Claymore is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Longswords|Longsword]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Dark Claymore can be purchased for 150,000,000 [[Coins]] from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Catacombs - Master Mode Floor VII]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|150,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.072% (1 in 1389.2 runs)|0.1181% (1 in 846.6 runs)|360|10|
### Raffle of the Century
Dark Claymore can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Sword:
Dark Claymore [[Damage]]: +500 [[Strength]]: +100 [[Crit Damage]]: +100 [[Swing Range]]: +2
The Dark Claymore, being a [[Longswords|Longsword]], sports +2 [[Swing Range]]: this is the most [[Swing Range]] granted by any Longsword in the game.

|✪✪|300|450|

|✪✪✪✪|900; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,850; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|1,500; 25,000 [[Coins]]|3,350; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Gallery
- In-hand model.
- In-hand render.
## Trivia
- The Dark Claymore closely resembles the [[Giant's Sword]]: however, it trades the Giant's Slam ability for increased [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], and [[Swing Range]].
- The text in Dark Claymore's tooltip is likely a reference to the Dragon Slayer from Berserk, described as "too big to be called a sword... Massive, thick, heavy, and far too rough. Indeed, it was like a heap of raw iron."
- The Dark Claymore was both the first item to have the stat [[Swing Range]] as well as the first [[Longswords|Longsword]].
- The usage requirement used to be bugged, only requiring a [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]] completion.
- The term Claymore refers to either the Scottish variant of a longsword or the Scottish variant of basket-hilted swords, the latter of which is sometimes also known as a broadsword.
- The Dark Claymore is one of two items other than [[Master Star|Master Stars]] and [[Master Skull|Master Skulls]] that are exclusive to Master Mode, the other being the [[Spirit Mask]].

Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics, Dark Auction

Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_DARK_PURPLE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #301934!; LEGENDARY DYE
Dark Purple Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
A Dark Purple Dye has a base 0.25% (1 in 400) chance of appearing in Rounds 1, 3, or 4 of the [[Dark Auction]] with a starting price of 20,000,000 [[Coins]].
The chance of appearing of this [[Dyes|Dye]] may be temporarily doubled or tripled by [[Vincent]] to become 0.5% or 0.75% respectively.
## Usage
Dark Purple Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #301934. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

The Deathripper Dagger is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer VI (6)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer VI (6) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Mawdredge Dagger]] 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 16x [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] 64x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] x16 + [[Molten Powder]] x64 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Mawdredge Dagger]] → Deathripper Dagger|
## Usage
Sword:
Deathripper Dagger [[Damage]]: +160 [[Strength]]: +75 [[Crit Chance]]: +10 [[Crit Damage]]: +25 [[Attack Speed]]: +20
[Effects]
[Deal 3.5x damage to Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs.; Deal 2x damage to Skeletal mobs.; Gain +45 Combat Wisdom against Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs. Ability: Attunement ON RIGHT CLICK; Swap between Spirit and Crystal mode in order to attack through Hellion Shields. Ability: Wraith Drain; On hit, heal for 200 + 4% Health after 6s if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement.]
The Deathripper Dagger deals 3.5x damage to [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs and 2x damage to [[Skeletal]] mobs. Additionally, it provides +45 [[Combat Wisdom]] against [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs.
The Deathripper Dagger has 2 abilities:
- Attunement allows the wielder to swap between Spirit and Crystal attunements in order to attack through the [[Inferno Demonlord]]'s Hellion Shield ability. When switching attunement, the Deathripper Dagger changes appearance from a Iron Sword to a Golden Sword.
- Wraith Drain allows the wielder to heal 200 [[Health]] + 4% after 6s upon hit if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement.

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Hilt of Revelations]] 1x [[Braided Griffin Feather]] 1x [[Archaic Spade]] 16x [[Mythos Fragment]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] 128x [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Ancient Claw]] x64 ×2 + [[Braided Griffin Feather]] + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x64 ×2 + [[Archaic Spade]] + [[Mythos Fragment]] x8 ×2 + [[Hilt of Revelations]] → Deific Spade|
## Usage
Item:
Deific Spade
[Effects]
[Use to reveal and dig up Griffin Burrows in the Hub (Hub Island) while Diana Diana's Mythological Ritual is active.; Maximum Burrow Chain Length: 8; Ability: Echo RIGHT CLICK; Show the way to the next or nearby Griffin burrow.; Cooldown: 1s]
The Deific Spade's Echo ability points the player towards the nearest [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] when used on the [[Hub Island]]. It can be also used to dig out burrows by left clicking them.
The available loot from treasure burrows is as follows, depending on the tier of the spade. It does not depend on the rarity of the player's [[Griffin Pet]].

|[[Braided Griffin Feather]]|[[Braided Griffin Feather]]|1|0.03% (1 in 3,221 treasures)|0.0068% (1 in 14,724.57 burrows)|0.0070% (1 in 14,315.56 burrows)|
|[[Myth the Fish]]|[[Myth the Fish]]||0.01% (1 in 10,000 treasures)|0.0022% (1 in 45,714.29 burrows)|0.0022% (1 in 44,444.44 burrows)|
## Trivia
- The maximum length of a [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] chain achievable with the Deific Spade is 8, or 10 with the [[Reforging|Erudite]] reforge when [[Daedalus' Notes]] is applied to it.
- When using the Deific Spade's ability, there is a 1% chance to play a random game sound instead of the usual sound, likely as an Easter egg.

# Devourer
Categories: Legendary items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Devourer]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DEVOURER
Tooltip: A relentless plant that spreads its root far and wide, consuming and destroying everything in its path.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; LEGENDARY MUTATION
A Devourer is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Devourer can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Puffercloud]] and 4 [[Zombud]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Devourer needs water and has 16 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases yield by 30%, allows for additional items to be found, and reduces water by -30%.
A Devourer has a 40% chance to spread roots upon reaching the next Growth Stage and destroy nearby crops.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Devourers also drop 1,805 (28 stacks plus 13) [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], 1,805 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]], 3,230 (50 stacks plus 30) [[Pumpkin]], and +3,400 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Devourer can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 10,000,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 5,000 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Devourer grants an additional 3,000 [[Copper]].
### NPC Trading
[[Devourer]] 1 Devourer is needed to purchase a [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Cocoaleech Shard]]|None|[[Devourer]] 1x Devourer 32x [[Designer Coffee Beans]]|Recipe: [[Designer Coffee Beans]] x4 ×8 + Devourer → [[Cocoaleech Shard]] x8|
## Gallery
- Devourer in the Greenhouse Devourer in the [[Greenhouse]]
- Devourer Roots in the Greenhouse Devourer Roots in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Tips
- Digging out the blocks directly adjacent to a Devourer setup will prevent it from destroying other crops on the plot. However, it can still destroy the mutations used to spawn it.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Devourer.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Devourer.|

Diamond Hunter Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set used for [[Fishing]].
## Obtaining
Diamond Hunter Armor can be obtained from [[Odger]] after reaching [[Odger|Master Trophy Fisher]] rank. Once obtained, it can be purchased from Odger's [[Odger|Lost Rewards Shop]] for 2,500,000 [[Coins]] per piece.
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Diamond Hunter Helmet ([[Diamond Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +125|Diamond Hunter Chestplate ([[Diamond Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +175|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Diamond Hunter Leggings ([[Diamond Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +150|Diamond Hunter Boots ([[Diamond Hunter Armor]]); [[Health]]: +100|
[Total]
[Diamond Hunter Armor Health: +550]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Star Fisher; Grants +1Fishing Speed per ✪ Upgrade Star on your armor. Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty; Gain immunity to Sea Creatures but they are immune to you and you can no longer catch them.; Pieces Required: 2+ Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing; Grants +0 (1 Piece)➜20 (2 Pieces)➜25 (3 Pieces)➜30 (4 Pieces) Trophy Chance.]
Due to its Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty, players no longer catch [[Sea Creatures]] and cannot do damage to them. However, they also gain immunity to any damage from Sea Creatures. The Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing also increases the player's chances of catching Trophy Fish.

## Tips
- Since Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty and Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing only require 2 pieces to activate, [[Froggles]] and a [[Red Sweater]] can be worn when fishing for [[Trophy Frogs]].
## Trivia
- If players would like to loot share Sea Creatures, they can get the damage requirement and switch to Trophy Hunter Armor in order to not die to them. This is especially noticeable for Boss [[Sea Creatures]], such as [[Thunder]] and [[Lord Jawbus]], since they do a lot of damage.
  - Diamond Hunter Armor nullifies the effects of [[Sea Creature Chance]], without affecting the stat itself.

# Ditto Blob
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Bits Shop
[Ditto Blob]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 600 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DITTO_BLOB
Tooltip: Combine this blob in an anvil with any item which is a skinned skull to turn it into a decoration block which you can place on your island.; CAUTION! The item will blob away!; (The item will be destroyed); LEGENDARY COSMETIC
Ditto Blob is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item obtained from the [[Bits Shop]]. It allows the player to capture a skinned skull when combined in an [[Anvil]], which can then be placed on their [[Private Island]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Ditto Blob can be purchased from the [[Bits Shop]] for 600 [[Bits]].
## Usage
Ditto Blob can be combined with any skinned skull in an [[Anvil]] to capture the skull. The Ditto Blob can then be placed as a block on the [[Private Island]].
## Trivia
- Unlike other items that contain 'Ditto' in their name, the Ditto Blob is not obtained from the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]].
- When combining a Ditto Blob with a [[Pets|Pet]], the original block will be displayed as "Unknown Pet".
- The Ditto Blob can also be used on itself to make it placeable. Once combined it will show a different lore on the Ditto Blob saying: "The Ditto is very happy to be a block of... itself?" instead of ''The Ditto is very happy to be a block!''
- This item turned a bug that existed in older versions of the game, where skinned skulls were able to be placed on the private island, into a feature.
  - However, previously placed skulls will still drop the original item after being broken.
- The name "Ditto" probably originates from the Pokémon of the same name, which is known for its ability to transform.
  - The simplistic face and color also somewhat resembles Ditto.
- Old skull items that were changed to [[Paper]] after the resource pack update that are preserved as skulls through storage on the [[Private Island]] still can not be combined with the Ditto Blob even though it is still a minecraft:player_head in the advanced tooltip.

# Divan's Alloy
Aliases: Alloy, Divan Alloy, Divans Alloy, Project Alloy
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Crystal Hollows
[Divan's Alloy]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: The legendary Divan explored where no other man has set foot, and came back with this incredibly rare alloy.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIVAN_ALLOY
Tooltip: The legendary Divan explored where no other man has set foot, and came back with this incredibly rare alloy.; LEGENDARY
Divan's Alloy is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] material used in forging the [[Divan's Drill]].
## Obtaining
A Divan's Alloy has a 0.005463% (1 in 18,306.6 rolls) chance of appearing in a [[Crystal Nucleus|Crystal Nucleus Bundle]]. This equals a base chance of approximately 0.090131% (1 in 1,109.5 bundles) per bundle. This chance can be increased by the High Roller [[Essence Shops|Diamond Essence Shop]] perk, the [[Attributes|Bigger Box]] ( [[Hideoncave Shard]]) attribute, and the [[Mole Pet]]'s Nucleic Explorer perk, for a final chance of 0.104607% (1 in 956.0 bundles).
It is guaranteed to drop after [[Pity and RNG Meters|1,000,000 Crystal Nucleus XP]] (accessible via /hotm or /rng), which equals 1,000 Nucleus Runs (or 910 with [[Attributes|Pity X]] ( [[Daemon Shard]])).
Divan's Alloy can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].

## Trivia
- Successfully rolling for any [[Gemstone|Flawless Gemstone]] from a Nucleus Loot Bundle is much more common than [[Divan's Alloy]] Divan's Alloy, by combining their weights:
  Item: 1 Any Flawless Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Weight: 95, Chance per Roll: 0.030923% (1 in 3,233.8 rolls), Chance per Bundle (16.5 rolls): 0.510234% (1 in 196.0 bundles), Chance per Bundle (19.15 rolls): 0.592181% (1 in 168.9 bundles); Item: [[Divan's Alloy]] 1 Divan's Alloy, Weight: 17, Chance per Roll: 0.005534% (1 in 18,071.3 rolls), Chance per Bundle (16.5 rolls): 0.091305% (1 in 1,095.2 bundles), Chance per Bundle (19.15 rolls): 0.105969% (1 in 943.7 bundles)
- Contrary to popular belief, the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]]'s formula is indeed applied to all rolls.
- Prior to 0.26, the Divan's Alloy resembled a Block of Gold when Minecraft was still in Alpha.
### Old Dynamic System and Project Alloy
The drop chance was previously believed to be handled by a dynamic system. It was intended to have a base drop chance of 0.000125% (1 in 800,000 runs). This drop chance would apply in all [[Crystal Hollows]] lobbies. Every hour (3 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock days]]), this chance would be multiplied by 10, capping at ×100, or 0.0125% (1 in 8,000 runs). Once a player obtained a Divan's Alloy, this chance would be reset, and the cycle would repeat. Additionally, it would only roll once per bundle unlike all the other loot.
- This system did indeed exist in the game's code, which is where the information on the official wiki page came from. However, it was never actually in effect, as the drop chance was overwritten to be 0.1% (1 in 1,000 runs) elsewhere.
  - Prior to the official wiki acknowledging the discrepancy, a player-made research group called Project Alloy had already discovered it. The research noted that most drops occurred within the first few hours after a previous drop, which means that the dynamic drop system was never in effect. It was also discovered that even the highest claimed drop chance of 0.0125% (1 in 8,000 runs) was statistically impossible given their data that suggested an average of 0.0778% (1 in 1,285 runs).
- A command called /resetdivanchance was also visible to players. This was likely intended to be used by staff to manually reset the drop chance of the Divan's Alloy.
- The player was required to have at least 25 hours of playtime to drop a Divan's Alloy.

# Divan's Powder Coating
Aliases: Coating, Divan Powder, Divan powder, Divans Powder, Divans powder, Powder Coating
Categories: Legendary items, Items
[Divan's Powder Coating]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])

[Item Properties]

Auctionable: ✕ No

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 32x [[Glossy Gemstone]] 32x [[Refined Mineral]] 5x [[Divan Fragment]] 409,600x (6,400 stacks (409,600 items total)) [[Gold Ingot]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIVAN_POWDER_COATING
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; When applied to Armor of Divan, grants +10 Mining Fortune.; When applied to a drill, grants +500 Mining Speed.; Can only be applied to an item once.; A recreation of the coating found on Divan's tools from his greatest expeditions.; LEGENDARY
Divan's Powder Coating is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item used to grant [[Mining Fortune]] to [[Armor of Divan]] and [[Pendant of Divan]], or [[Mining Speed]] to [[Drills]].
## Obtaining
Divan's Powder Coating can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]]. It requires 32 [[Glossy Gemstone|Glossy Gemstones]], 32 [[Refined Mineral|Refined Minerals]], 5 [[Divan Fragment|Divan Fragments]], 16 [[Enchanted Gold Block|Enchanted Gold Blocks]], and 36 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Divan's Powder Coating]] 1 Divan's Powder Coating
  - 32 [[Glossy Gemstone]]
  - 32 [[Refined Mineral]]
  - 5 [[Divan Fragment]]
  - 16 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 409,600 (6,400 stacks) [[Gold Ingot]]
### Raffle of the Century
Divan's Powder Coating can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Item:
Divan's Powder Coating
[Effects]
[When applied to Armor of Divan Armor of Divan, grants +10 Mining Fortune.; When applied to a Drill (Drills), grants +500 Mining Speed.]
Divan's Powder Coating can be applied once per item, for the following:
- to [[Armor of Divan]] pieces and to [[Pendant of Divan]] to grant +10 [[Mining Fortune]] each
- to [[Drills]], granting +500 [[Mining Speed]];
- to [[Powder of the Many]], upgrading it into [[Powder of the People]].
## Trivia
- When Divan’s Powder Coating is applied to all four pieces of [[Armor of Divan]], golden particles appear around the player while worn.

The drop chance of this [[Dyes|Dye]] may be temporarily doubled or tripled by [[Vincent]].
The chance can also be increased with [[Overbloom]].
## Usage
Dung Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #4F2A2A. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

# Dungeon Orb
Aliases: Dungeon Stone
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Dungeons
[Dungeon Orb]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: When entering a Dungeon, this stone adapts to its user and allows them to use their class abilities.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DUNGEON_STONE
Tooltip: When entering a Dungeon, this stone adapts to its user and allows them to use their class abilities.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON ITEM
Dungeon Orb is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeon Items|Dungeon Item]] that adapts to a player's [[Classes|Class]] in [[Dungeons]], granting them special abilities.
## Obtaining
A Dungeon Orb is given by [[Mort]] on every [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] run of [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] and below if the player does not have a Dungeon Orb in their inventory. A Dungeon Orb may also be obtained by clicking in the bottom right of the class selection screen.
## Usage
Each [[Classes|Class]] has different abilities that can be activated using the Dungeon Orb and change the Dungeon Orb's texture and lore while in a [[Dungeons|Dungeon]]. Activating [[Classes|Class]] abilities do not require the player to have the Dungeon Orb in their inventory.
### Abilities
The Healer Dungeon Orb
- Healing Circle: Launches a healing orb that creates a healing circle where it lands. The healing circle heals friendly players inside for 2% of their health every second. Cooldown: 2 seconds
- Wish: Heals everyone in your group to full health and grants them an [[Absorption]] shield for 20%-40% of their max health. Cooldown: 120 seconds
The Mage Dungeon Orb
- Guided Sheep: Shoots a Guided Sheep, dealing damage based on your Mage level! Can destroy weak walls and crypts inside a dungeon. Cooldown: 30 seconds
- Thunderstorm: Unleash a Thunderstorm, striking enemies in a 10-block radius for 15 seconds dealing damage based on your Mage level! Cooldown: 500 seconds
The Berserk Dungeon Orb
- Throwing Axe: Throw an Axe, doing the damage of your highest damage hit in the last minute. Cooldown: 10 seconds
- Ragnarok: Summon 3 Zombie minions to aid you in battle! Cooldown: 60 seconds
The Archer Dungeon Orb
- Explosive Shot: Shoots 3 arrows that explode on impact, dealing the same damage as your highest Bow hit in the last minute in a 4 block radius. Can also explode things similarly to [[Superboom TNT]]. Cooldown: 40 seconds
- Rapid Fire: Shoots 5 Arrows per second for 4 seconds. Arrows deal 75% of your highest Bow hit in the last minute. Cooldown: 100 seconds
The Tank Dungeon Orb
- Seismic Wave: A Seismic Wave emerges from underneath you and travels in a straight line. Deals 20,000-1,420,000 + 10% every 50 [[Defense]] in damage to any enemy in its path. Cooldown: 15 seconds
- Castle of Stone: Fortifies your Defense by giving a 70% damage reduction for 20 seconds. Also aggros all enemies in a 10 block radius. Cooldown: 150 seconds
## History

|Jan 30, 2026|Added Dungeon Orb to [[Museum]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Added Dungeon Orb to [[Museum]].|

Tooltip: Consumable; The first 5 times you consume this, gain a permanent +2 Dwarven Metal Fortune.; Right-click to consume!; LEGENDARY
Dwarven O's Metallic Minis are a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Consumables|Consumable]]. They are obtained from looting [[Frozen Corpses]] in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
## Obtaining
Dwarven O's Metallic Minis have a 0.31% chance to drop from Umber and Tungsten [[Frozen Corpses]], and a 3.41% chance to drop from Vanguard Corpses.
## Usage
Dwarven O's Metalic Minis can be consumed up to 5 times. Each Dwarven O's Metallic Minis eaten permanently increases the player's [[Dwarven Metal Fortune]] by +2 , up to +10 [[Dwarven Metal Fortune]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Random Message|
|-|-|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, rock and stone!|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, for Karl!|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, they're magically delicious!|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, they're grrreat!|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|Haven't you already had enough?|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|You can't seem to get the packaging off this one...|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|I don't think I should go over my calorie limit for today.|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|I think you should leave some for the rest of the players.|
## Trivia
- The messages for eating Dwarven O's Metallic Minis are each references.
  - Both "rock and stone" and "for Karl!" reference Deep Rock Galactic.
  - "They're magically delicious!" is the tagline for Lucky Charms.
  - "They're grrreat!!" is the tagline for Frosted Flakes.

The Eccentric Painting is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Bizarre]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Intelligence]] but also negative [[Crit Damage]] and [[Strength]]. It also provides +5 [[Ability Damage]] as its unique power bonus.
## Obtaining
Eccentric Paintings are obtained from opening a [[Eccentric Painting Bundle]], which is sold at the [[Dark Auction]] for a minimum bid of 20,000,000 [[Coins]] and when opened, give 9 Eccentric Paintings.
## Requirements
The Eccentric Painting has a [[Combat|Combat XXV (25)]] requirement and is only used as a [[Power Stones|Power Stone]] for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9x Eccentric Paintings at [[Maxwell]] will unlock the Bizarre power permanently.

# Eleanor's Armor

Tooltip: Rift Time: +20s; Tiered Bonus: Riches (0\ 4); Gain 1.5x Scavenger Coins.; Scavenger Coins Gained: 0; Delicately crafted from the finest enchanted silk, this cap combines elegance with subtlety.; * Soulbound * X Rift-Transferable X LEGENDARY HELMET
Eleanor's Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set, obtainable on [[The Mountaintop]] of the [[Rift Dimension]] by doing a series of quests.
## Obtaining
### Helmet
Players must speak to [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth V]] in [[The Mountaintop]] to receive a quest, after which, they'll receive [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Cap]].
To complete the quest, the player must complete the following steps:
- Wearing a full Armor set of [[Cropie Armor]], [[Squash Armor]], [[Fermento Armor]] or [[Helianthus Armor]]
  - Though these armor sets are listed as requiring certain [[Farming]] skill levels to use (ex. Cropie Armor requires [[Farming|Farming XXX (30)]]), they can still be worn and used for this quest even if the player's Farming level is below the threshold.
- Equipping an [[Elephant Pet]] or [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] of [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity or higher
- Holding one of the following items:
  - [[Euclid's Wheat Sickle]], [[Gauss Carrot Shovel]], [[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]], [[Newton Nether Wart Cutter|Newton Nether Warts Hoe]], [[Pythagorean Potato Shovel]], or one of their upgrades.
- Being on [[The Barn]] in the carrot field
  - Within the coordinates [[Coordinates|134, 81, -282]] and [[Coordinates|121, 88, -291]]
- Say Eleanor is the best!
Once completed, the message "You sense a watchful presence..." will appear in chat, signalling that the player may now claim it in the [[Rift Dimension]] and transfer it outside of it.
### Chestplate
Players must speak to [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth IX]] in [[The Mountaintop]] to receive a quest, after which, they'll receive [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Tunic]].
To complete the quest, the player must complete the following steps:
- Wearing one of the following full Armor Sets:
  - [[Sponge Armor]], [[Shark Scale Armor]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], [[Nutcracker Armor]]
  - [[Gold Hunter Armor]][Confirm], [[Diamond Hunter Armor]][Confirm],
  - [[Diver's Armor]], [[Abyssal Armor]]
  - [[Thunder Armor|Thunder Armorwith]] [[Thunderbolt Necklace]]
  - [[Magma Lord Armor]][Confirm], with [[Magma Lord Necklace]][Confirm].
- Equipping a [[Flying Fish Pet]] or [[Ammonite Pet]] of [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity or higher.
- Holding any [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
- Being inside the [[Dwarven Mines]] while within the coordinates [[Coordinates|-130, 195, -94]] and [[Coordinates|-172, 214, -49]].
  - The location is in a cave that can be accessed at [[Coordinates|-121, 205, -60]].
- Splashing themselves with any potion.
Once completed, the message "You sense a watchful presence..." will appear in chat, signalling that the player may now claim it in the [[Rift Dimension]] and transfer it outside of it.
### Leggings
Players must speak to [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth VII]] in [[The Mountaintop]] to receive a quest, after which, they'll receive [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Trousers]].
To complete the quest, the player must complete the following steps:
- Wearing a full set of any golden Armor.
  - Applicable armor sets: [[Armor of Divan]], [[Diver's Armor]], [[Golden Armor]] and [[Gold Hunter Armor]]
- Equipping a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Ocelot Pet]]
- Standing near the far corner of the [[Jungle Island]] of [[The Park]]
  - Around the coordinates [[Coordinates|-469, 127, -84]]
Once completed, the message "You sense a watchful presence..." will appear in chat, signalling that the player may now claim it in the [[Rift Dimension]] and transfer it outside of it.
### Boots
Players must speak to [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I]] in [[The Mountaintop]] to receive a quest, after which, they'll receive [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Slippers]].
To complete the quest, the player must complete the following steps:
- Wear a full set of [[Heat Armor]], [[Flamebreaker Armor]], [[Sorrow Armor]], or [[Armor of Divan]].
- Equip a [[Armadillo Pet|Armadillo]], [[Mithril Golem Pet|Mithril Golem]], [[Scatha Pet|Scatha]], [[Silverfish Pet|Silverfish]], or [[Snail Pet|Snail]] Pet.
- Hold a [[Drills|Drill]] of any type.
- Be between [[Coordinates|32, 64, 27]] and [[Coordinates|52, 75, 3]] in the [[Slimehill]].
- Sneak twice within one second.
Once completed, a message will appear in chat. The player can then return to [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth I]] to claim [[Eleanor's Armor|Eleanor's Slippers]].

## Trivia
- Eleanor's Armor is the only full set of [[Armor]] to be [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
- [[Coins]] obtained by materializing from a [[Ghost]]'s death are not counted as "Scavenged" on the armor.
- Eleanor's Armor shares the Color ⁠ #FFD700 with [[Haymaker Armor|Farm Armor]] and [[W Logo]].

## Obtaining
The Elegant Tuxedo can be purchased from [[Seymour]] for 74,999,999 [[Coins]] in the [[Fashion Shop]].
A [[Fancy Tuxedo]] cannot be upgraded into an Elegant Tuxedo.
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Chestplate|Leggings|
|-|-|
|Elegant Tuxedo Jacket ([[Elegant Tuxedo]]); [[Crit Damage]]: +100 [[Intelligence]]: +300|Elegant Tuxedo Pants ([[Elegant Tuxedo]]); [[Crit Damage]]: +50 [[Intelligence]]: +100|
|Boots||
|Elegant Tuxedo Oxfords ([[Elegant Tuxedo]]); [[Crit Damage]]: +50 [[Intelligence]]: +100 [[Speed]]: +10||
[Total]
[Elegant Tuxedo Crit Damage: +200 Intelligence: +500 Speed: +10]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Dashing!; Max health set to 250 Health. Deal +150% more Damage!]
## Tips
- The Elegant Tuxedo allows one to deal massive damage, but its penalty to survivability renders it mostly useless for most combat content available.
- The armor can often be bought off of the Auction House for cheaper then the actual set NPC cost. This can be useful for players that want armor with enchants on it, as well as for cheaper.
## Trivia
- Wearing the full set awards the player with the [[Achievements|Glass Cannon]] [[Achievements|Achievement]].
- The damage from the full set bonus is part of the [[Damage Calculation|additive multiplier]], hence the actual damage increase is lower in percentage.
- The Elegant Tuxedo Jacket and Oxfords shares the Color ⁠ #191919 with [[Leggings of the Coven]]'s black variant.

# Emerald Dye
Aliases: Emerald Dye (Old)
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Emerald Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_EMERALD
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #50C878!; LEGENDARY DYE
Emerald Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
An Emerald Dye can be obtained from regular [[Critters]] and [[Critter Safari|SPARKLING Critters]], with the drop chance varying based on the Critter rarity.
|Critter Rarity|Chance Normal|Chance SPARKLING ([[Critter Safari]])|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|0.0002% (1 in 500,000)|0.02% (1 in 5,000)|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|0.0004% (1 in 250,000)|0.04% (1 in 2,500)|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|0.001% (1 in 100,000)|0.1 (1 in 1,000)|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|0.002% (1 in 50,000)|0.2% (1 in 500)|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|0.004% (1 in 25,000)|0.4% (1 in 250)|
## Usage
Emerald Dye can be applied to any vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #50C878. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.

- Emerald Bucket of Dye Emerald [[Bucket of Dye]]

# Enchanted Clock
Aliases: Enchanted Time Clock
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Craftable items, Pages with UIs, Aging Items, Tools, Other Tools, Enchanted
[Enchanted Clock]
Type: Item

Collection ([[Collections]]): Gold Ingot X (10) ([[Gold Ingot]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1,638,400x (25,600 stacks (1,638,400 items total)) [[Gold Ingot]] 1,638,400x [[Redstone Dust]] 2,048x (32 stacks (2,048 items total)) [[Youngite]] 2,048x [[Timite]] 1,024x (16 stacks (1,024 items total)) [[Obsolite]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_TIME_CLOCK
Tooltip: Ability: Time Warp RIGHT CLICK Advance time by 1 hour!; Cooldown: 48 hours; Impacts your Minions, Chocolate Factory, Aging Items, Kat, Fann, Greenhouse, and The Forge.; LEGENDARY
The Enchanted Clock is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item used to speed up the time of various events by 1 hour.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Gold Ingot X (10) ([[Gold Ingot]])|64x [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 64x [[Highlite]] 64x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x32 ×2 + [[Highlite]] x32 ×2 + [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] x64 → Enchanted Clock|
## Usage
Item:
Enchanted Clock
[Effects]
[Ability: Time Warp RIGHT CLICK; Advance time by 1 hour!; Impacts your Minions, Chocolate Factory, Aging Items, Kat, Fann, Greenhouse, and The Forge.; Cooldown: 48h]
When used, the Enchanted Clock gives the player the option to advance either [[Minions]], [[Chocolate Factory]], [[Fann]] Timer, [[Kat]] Timer, [[Time Pocket|Aging Items]], [[The Forge|Forge]], or [[Greenhouse]]. Each of those can be activated separately from all others, after which that particular option goes on cooldown for 48 hours. If a member of one's co-op uses this item to speed up Minions, it goes on cooldown for all of the co-op's members. If the Kat, Fann or Forge slots are all empty, or if there are no crops planted in any Greenhouse plots, then those options cannot be activated. The Minions boost can only be used if the player is on their [[Private Island]], and the Greenhouse boost can only be used if the player is on [[The Garden]].
## Notes
- Using Enchanted Clock on Chocolate Factory while Time Tower perk is active will not affect its duration or cooldown.
- Using this item on Minions results in offline calculations being performed, however any minion buffs provided by [[Pets]] also apply if the player is on the island and has them equipped.
- Using this item on Minions with temporary fuels will remove 1 hour of its duration.
- The [[Time Pocket]] doubles the 1 hour time skip granted by this item.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Using Time Warp|TIME WARP! You have successfully warped time for your <time warp type>!|
|Attempting to use a Time Warp that is on cooldown|TIME NOPE! This action is currently on cooldown! (Cooldown: <remaining time>)|
|Attempting to use Minions Time Warp outside of your [[Private Island]]|TIME NOPE! You need to be on your private island to use this!|
|Attempting to use Pet Training Time Warp with no active training sessions|TIME NOPE! Didn't time warp any Training Sessions- you don't have any!|
|Attempting to use Pet Sitting Time Warp without a pet being taken care of|TIME NOPE! You have no pet being taken care of by Kat- so no time warp!|
|Attempting to use Pet Sitting Time Warp when pet is finished|TIME NOPE! Your pet being taken care of by Kat has already finished upgrading its rarity!|
|Attempting to use Forge Time Warp with no items in the Forge|TIME NOPE! You didn't have any active Forge Items to speed up!|
|Attempting to use Greenhouse Time Warp outside of [[The Garden]]|TIME NOPE! You need to be on your Garden to use this!|
|Attempting to use Greenhouse Time Warp with nothing planted|TIME NOPE! Nothing is currently growing in your Greenhouse. Plant something first!|

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Dec 13, 2024|Added Gold Ingot X (10) ([[Gold Ingot]]) requirement to craft.|
|Sep 1, 2026|0.27.1|Added boost for the [[Greenhouse]].|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENDSTONE_IDOL
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; When applied to a weapon, increases its Ender Slayer level by 1!; Can be applied once.; Requires Ender Slayer VI!; LEGENDARY
An End Stone Idol is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrade]] that can be applied to any [[Weapons|Weapon]] with [[Ender Slayer|Ender Slayer VI]] to upgrade it to [[Ender Slayer|Ender Slayer VII]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
An End Stone Idol has a 0.014% chance to drop from the Tier IV [[Voidgloom Seraph]] if the player is [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer VII (7)]] or above, and can be guaranteed upon reaching 3,542,250 XP in [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]].
### Experimentation Table
An End Stone Idol also has a 0.0263% chance to be obtained from [[Experiments|Superpairs]], and can be guaranteed upon reaching 500,000 XP in [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]].
### Legacy Trades
The player can trade in an 1 [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Ender Slayer|Ender Slayer VII]]) for an End Stone Idol.
## Usage
An End Stone Idol can be applied in an anvil to any weapon that has [[Ender Slayer|Ender Slayer VI]] in order to upgrade it to [[Ender Slayer|Ender Slayer VII]].

## Obtaining
### Hunting
An End Stone Protector Shard can be obtained from killing an [[End Stone Protector]] with at least 240 quality or with enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
An End Stone Protector Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the End Stone Protector Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Unlimited Fortitude]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Unlimited Fortitude ([[Attributes]]); Your [[Defense]] is increased by +0.2%–2%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

The Endercat Enderman Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Endercat Enderman Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 28, 2026. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Endercat Enderman Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Enderman Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 14 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 14 ender themed cats!

Tooltip: Unlimited Energy I Your  Crit Damage is increased by +0.1%.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 20.; LEGENDARY COMBAT SHARD (ID L39)
The Etherdrake Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Unlimited Energy]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
An Etherdrake Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Apex Dragon Shard]]
- Second: [[Kraken Shard]]
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Etherdrake Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Unlimited Energy]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Unlimited Energy ([[Attributes]]); Your [[Crit Damage]] is increased by +0.1%–1%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An Etherdrake Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Etherdrake Shard has the following description:
- This ethereal dragon wields the power of the afterlife, its breath a chilling mist that drains the life force of anything it touches.

The Fancy Tuxedo can be purchased from [[Seymour]] for 20,000,000 [[Coins]] in the [[Fashion Shop]].

- This set has no [[Helmets|Helmet]], so you can add any [[Helmets|Helmet]] to the set and maintain the set bonus.

# Fang-tastic Chocolate Chip
Aliases: Crumbly

[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHOCOLATE_CHIP
Tooltip: Power Stone Combine 9x of this stone at the Thaumaturgist to permanently unlock the Crumbly power.; At 1,000 Magical Power:; +326 Health +53 True Defense +16 Crit Chance +23 Attack Speed +174 Intelligence; Unique Power Bonus:; +25 Speed; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 20.; X Rift-Exportable X Loses motes value when crossing dimensions.; LEGENDARY POWER STONE
Fang-tastic Chocolate Chip is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Crumbly]] power for [[Accessories]]. The item is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Exportable #]].
## Obtaining
The Fang-tastic Chocolate Chip is a [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drop from the Tier II [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] or above if the player has [[Vampire Slayer|Vampire Slayer LVL III (3)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier II: 15%
## Requirements
The Fang-tastic Chocolate Chip requires [[Combat|Combat XX (20)]] to be used and can only be used as a Power Stone for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Fang-tastic Chocolate Chips at [[Maxwell]] will permanently unlock the Crumbly [[Power Stones|Power]] for [[Accessories]].

## Tips
- Despite giving less [[True Defense]] than the [[Bubba Blister|Bubba Power]], it's better for surviving against the [[Inferno Demonlord]] due to Crumbly not reducing [[Defense]].
  - Although it does not not grant any damage stats, so while it will make the Slayer a lot easier, slaying it will take a while.
## Trivia
- It is the only Accessory Power to have given [[Mending]] at some point, although this has since been removed.

Tooltip: Gear Score: 367 (404); Damage: +275 (+302.5); Crit Damage: +115% (+126.5%); Swing Range: +1 (+1); Ability: Reaving Strike RIGHT CLICK Slash in a huge arc, dealing 135% melee damage to all enemies hit!; Deals +1% damage for every 1% of missing health on the target.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VI Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON LONGSWORD
The Felthorn Reaper is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Longswords|Longsword]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Fel Skull]] 1x [[Bone Reaver]] 5x [[Fel Rose]]|Recipe: [[Fel Rose]] ×5 + [[Fel Skull]] + [[Bone Reaver]] → Felthorn Reaper|
## Usage
Sword:
Felthorn Reaper [[Damage]]: +275 [[Crit Damage]]: +115 [[Swing Range]]: +1
[Effects]
[Deals +1% damage for every 1% of missing health on the target. Ability: Reaving Strike RIGHT CLICK; Slash in a huge arc, dealing 135% melee damage to all enemies hit!; Charges: 5 / 4s]
Sword:
Felthorn Reaper (fragged) ([[Felthorn Reaper]]) [[Damage]]: +285 [[Strength]]: +10 [[Crit Damage]]: +125 [[Swing Range]]: +1.5
[Effects]
[Deals +1% damage for every 1% of missing health on the target. Ability: Reaving Strike RIGHT CLICK; Slash in a huge arc, dealing 135% melee damage to all enemies hit!; Charges: 5 / 4s]
The Felthorn Reaper, being a [[Longswords|Longsword]], grants +1 [[Swing Range]]: this can be increased to +1.5 [[Swing Range]] when upgraded. It also deals +1% more damage to mobs per 1% of missing health on the target.
The Felthorn Reaper's Reaving Strike allows the wielder to slash in an arc: this slash deals +135% of the wielder's melee damage to all mobs that are hit. This ability has 5 charges: each charge recharges every 4s.
## Upgrading
The Felthorn Reaper can be upgraded using [[Bigfoot's Bola|Bigfoot's Bolas]] to buff its stats, granting +10 [[Damage]], +10 [[Strength]], +10% [[Crit Damage]], and +0.5 [[Swing Range]].
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Felthorn Reaper]] 1x Felthorn Reaper 8x [[Bigfoot's Bola]]|Recipe: [[Bigfoot's Bola]] ×8 + Felthorn Reaper → [[Felthorn Reaper]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|20|20|
|✪✪|50|70|
|✪✪✪|100|170|
|✪✪✪✪|200; 10,000 [[Coins]]|370; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|300; 25,000 [[Coins]]|670; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- The Reaving Strike ability can damage mobs that the [[Flower of Truth]]'s Heat-Seeking Rose and the [[Bouquet of Lies]]' Petal Barrage abilities cannot, such as the Tier V [[Revenant Horror|Atoned Horror]], [[Sven Packmaster]], and [[Ghost|Ghosts]].
## Trivia
- Unlike its predecessor, the Felthorn Reaper is called a Reaper instead of a Reaver.

Tooltip: Health: +180; Defense: +100; True Defense: +5; Intelligence: +15; Gemstones: [⚔] [⚔]; Tiered Bonus: Fervor (0\ 4); Every 1.5s, attacking a mob grants 1 stack of ҉ Fervor. Lose 1 stack after 4s of not gaining a stack.; Ability: Ground Pound SNEAK At 10 stacks, sneak to reset your stacks and perform a Ground Pound dealing damage to mobs within 6 blocks. (Damage scales with your EHP, max 25M).; Full Set Bonus: Intimidate (0\ 4); Attacking a mob has a 50% chance to direct aggro from 3 mobs to you.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; ❣ Requires Kuudra Basic Tier Completion.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Fervor Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set. There are 4 upgraded tiers of Fervor Armor: Hot, Burning, Fiery, and Infernal, which can all be obtained by prestiging the previous tier.
## Obtaining
Basic Tier Fervor Armor Pieces can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Paid Reward Chests]] at the end of a [[Kuudra]] fight.
Piece Drop Chances:
- Basic Tier: 4.31%
- Hot Tier: 4.27%
- Burning Tier: 4.18%
- Fiery Tier: 4.1%
- Infernal Tier: 4.01%
Higher tiers of Fervor Armor can be obtained by prestiging the previous tier at a blacksmith ( [[Malik]], [[Igor]], [[Alwin]], or [[The Hex]]), which can be done if it has 10 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] applied to it. After being prestiged, Armor pieces lose their Stars.

The number of Ground Pounds and their range are determined by the lowest tier of Fervor Armor worn:
|Lowest Tier Worn|Ground Pounds|Range|
|-|-|-|
|Basic|1|6 blocks|
|Hot|1|7 blocks|
|Burning|2|8 blocks|
|Fiery|2|9 blocks|
|Infernal|2|10 blocks|
The damage of each Ground Pound scales with the player's [[Effective Health]] and is capped at 25000000 [[Damage]] per Ground Pound.

## Trivia
- It has been observed by some community members that the usage of the word "Stacks" and how they function could be a potential reference to the Risk of Rain series.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- A total of 141,005 (2,203 stacks plus 13) [[Crimson Essence]], 45 [[Heavy Pearl]], 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Kuudra Teeth]], and 44,740,000 [[Coins]] are required for one Infernal tier piece, costing .
  - A total of 1,793,505 (28,023 stacks plus 33) [[Crimson Essence]], 97 (1 stack plus 33) [[Heavy Pearl]], 460 (7 stacks plus 12) [[Kuudra Teeth]], and 139,175,000 [[Coins]] are needed from scratch for a single Infernal piece with 15 [[Star Upgrades|Stars]], costing .

Final Destination Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set. It is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer III (3)]] and is geared towards [[Enderman Slayer]].

The Enderman Bulwark piece bonus grants [[Defense]] against [[Enderman|Endermen]], which increases with [[Enderman]] kills and caps at +400 [[Defense]] per piece, or +1600 [[Defense]] in total. ## Tips
- Final Destination Armor is often compared to [[Crimson Armor|Burning Crimson Armor]]: while [[Crimson Armor|Burning Crimson Armor]] is superior in terms of raw damage, Final Destination Armor is significantly better for survivability.
  - If the player is able to survive a Tier II [[Voidgloom Seraph]], then [[Crimson Armor|Burning Crimson Armor]] is recommended.
## Trivia
- Final Destination Armor is a reference to the Final Destination franchise.

# Flame Dye
Aliases: Flame Dye (Old)
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Cosmetics
[Flame Dye]
Type: Dye ([[Dyes]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DYE_FLAME
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #E25822!; LEGENDARY DYE
Flame Dye is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
A Flame Dye can be obtained from killing any tier of the [[Inferno Demonlord]]. The drop chance depends on what tier of the slayer boss is killed. This chance is not affected by [[Magic Find]].
|Slayer Tier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Tier I|0.00001% (1 in 10,000,000)|
|Tier II|0.00004% (1 in 2,500,000)|
|Tier III|0.0001% (1 in 1,000,000)|
|Tier IV|0.0002% (1 in 500,000)|
## Usage
Flame Dye can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece, excluding [[Armor/Helmets|skulls]], by combining the two items in an [[Anvil]] to change the hex color of that piece to ⁠ #E25822.
It can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] with a texture unique to its color.
## Gallery
- Flame Dyed Leather Armor
- Flame Bucket of Dye Flame [[Bucket of Dye]]

Tooltip: Damage: +200; Strength: +250; Ferocity: +25; Gemstones: [⚔] [⚔]; Deals 3x damage to Sea Creatures.; Ability: Flay RIGHT CLICK Flay your whip in an arc, dealing your melee damage to all enemies in its path. Heal 1%  for each Sea Creature hit, up to 25 .; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 47.; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Flaming Flay is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XLVII (47)]] to use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Soul Whip]] 1x [[Hotspot Water Orb]] 1x [[Brimstone Handle]] 1x [[Braided Griffin Feather]] 1x [[Lava Water Orb]]|Recipe: [[Braided Griffin Feather]] + [[Soul Whip]] + [[Hotspot Water Orb]] + [[Brimstone Handle]] + [[Lava Water Orb]] → Flaming Flay|
## Usage
Sword:
Flaming Flay [[Damage]]: +200 [[Strength]]: +250 [[Ferocity]]: +25
[Effects]
[Deals 3x damage to Sea Creatures. Ability: Flay RIGHT CLICK; Flay your whip in an arc, dealing your melee damage to all enemies in its path. Heal 1% Health for each Sea Creature (Sea Creatures) hit, up to 25 Health .]
The Flaming Flay can be used as a melee or ranged weapon.
When the Flay ability is used, a red and yellow beam is shot in the direction the player is aiming. This beam has a parabolic trajectory, traveling forward 13 blocks and upwards 2 blocks. The beam travels through enemies, damaging all in its path; for the first 3 enemies hit, it deals its melee damage, then it is halved for every subsequent enemy hit. Each hit [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] heals 1% [[Health]] . The ability does not consume [[Mana]], but has a hidden cooldown of 0.5s. Holding right-click will continuously activate the ability.
The Flaming Flay also deals 3x damage to [[Sea Creatures]].
## Essence Crafting
The Flaming Flay can be upgraded with [[Wither Essence]]. Once converted, it requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XXIII (23)]] to use.
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|300|300|
|✪|200|500|
|✪✪|300|800|
|✪✪✪|400|1,200|
|✪✪✪✪|550; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,750; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|750; 25,000 [[Coins]]|2,500; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- Due to its high piercing and range, it has good crowd-control capabilities.
- This weapon, along with the [[Soul Whip]] and [[Halberd of the Shredded]], can be used to take [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Wither Lords']] aggro easily.
## Trivia
- Melee-only enchantments, such [[Life Steal]], [[Mana Steal]], [[Sharpness]], and [[Drain]], work on the beam.
- When right-clicking with this weapon, the [[Autopet]] rule for throwing a fishing hook does not trigger.
## Bugs
- While [[Power Scrolls]] can be applied to a Flaming Flay, its ability does not activate them.
- Dealing the killing blow to certain [[Sea Creatures]] that consist of multiple entities (such as [[Pyroclastic Worm|Pyroclastic Worms]]) using the Flaming Flay's ability may result in no loot being dropped.
- When attacking certain mobs with the Flaming Flay's ability, extra damage numbers may appear, even though the ability only hit once.

[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Flex Helmet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Helmets|Helmet]]. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
The Flex Helmet can be bid on at [[Barrier Street]]. The top 15 bidders win the item.
## Usage
The Flex Helmet can be worn as a [[Helmets|Helmet]]. Whilst in the [[Rift Dimension]], it costs 100 [[Motes]] per second to wear. Outside the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], it costs 1,000 [[Coins]] per second. The item displays the amount of time worn.
Attempting to use this helmet without the required 100 [[Motes]] or 1,000 [[Coins]] will display a message saying:
WEIRD FLEX! Unequipped Flex Helmet because you cannot afford it!
After this, the Flex Helmet will be automatically sent to inventory, or to stash if it's full.
### Interface
Right-clicking the Flex Helmet opens the following interface, which allows the player to select an animal skin.
## Trivia
- The Dog skin shares a texture with the [[Mastiff Armor|Mastiff Crown]].
- Once modified, it cannot be sent back to the [[Rift Dimension]]. This item has been upgraded, enchanted, or modified! It cannot be transferred over to The Rift.

Tooltip: Unique Bonus I Counts as +1 towards the Unique Crop Bonus in your Greenhouse.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 20.; LEGENDARY FOREST SHARD (ID L52)
The Flora Shard is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Unique Bonus]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Greenhouse
Contrary to its hunting method's description, obtaining a Flora Shard is not random. Instead, the player is guaranteed to obtain 1 per 3333,3 [[Copper]] gained, which can be simplified into 0.03 shard per 100 [[Copper]] obtained from analyzing [[Mutations]] using the [[Crop Analyzer]]. Multiple Flora Shards can drop from analyzing the same [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Flora Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Unique Bonus]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Unique Bonus ([[Attributes]]); Counts as +1-10 towards the Unique Crop Bonus in your [[Greenhouse]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|1|3|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|5|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|2|7|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|2|9|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|3|12|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|3|15|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|4|19|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|5|24|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Flora Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Flora Shard has the following description:
- This spirit of plant diversity takes form whenever a mutation is analyzed, carrying the memory of every unique crop it has ever known.
## Trivia
- It takes exactly 80,000 [[Copper]] in the [[Crop Analyzer]] to max the Flora Shard.
  - This results in 160,000,000 [[Coins]] (or 100,000,000 [[Coins]] with maxed [[Garden Chips|Synthesis Chip]] and [[Rose Dragon Pet]]) spent on the [[Crop Analyzer]].
    - The [[Cheesebite]] cost to analyze is 1,000 [[Coins]] instead of 2,000 [[Coins]] per 1 [[Copper]], but it's usually not worth it, since the [[Bazaar]] cost is much higher than other [[Mutations]].
- The Flora Shard, along with the [[Lapis Zombie Shard]] and [[Miner Zombie Shard]], is one of few [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] to not use a player head.

The Floral Helianthus Helmet Skin can be purchased from the Spring 2026 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it costs 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]].

The Floral Helianthus Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Helianthus Armor|Helianthus Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 20 different versions.

Damage multiplies as more enemies are hit.; Health Cost: 100; Cooldown: 1s; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VI Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON SWORD
The Flower of Truth is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Dungeon [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|9x [[Ancient Rose]]|Recipe: [[Ancient Rose]] x3 ×3 → Flower of Truth|
## Usage
Sword:
Flower of Truth [[Damage]]: +160 [[Strength]]: +300
[Effects]
[Ability: Heat-Seeking Rose RIGHT CLICK; Shoots a rose that ricochets between enemies, hitting up to 3 of your foes! Damage multiplies as more enemies are hit.; Health Cost (Health): 100; Cooldown: 1s]
The Flower of Truth's Heat-Seeking Rose ability shoots a rose that can travel up to 20 blocks before disappearing. After traveling 5 blocks, it homes in on enemies within 10 blocks. Upon hitting an enemy, it bounces to another enemy up to 2 more times, for a maximum of 3 times. The homing rose's damage is doubled on the first bounce and tripled on the final bounce. The rose is destroyed upon hitting a block.
The ability has a cooldown of 1s and consumes 100 [[Health]] on use. This ability cannot be used if the user does not have enough health to be consumed, so using it repeatedly cannot cause fatal damage. Unlike other abilities, the damage does not scale with [[Intelligence]] or [[Ability Damage]]: instead, it uses the same calculation as melee damage, including factoring in [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]].
[[Enchantments]] that do not affect abilities, such as [[Sharpness]], [[Life Steal]], [[Drain]], [[Cleave]], and [[Mana Steal]], as well as [[Ferocity]], do not work with the Heat-Seeking Rose ability.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bouquet of Lies]]|None|[[Flower of Truth]] 1x Flower of Truth 12x [[Fel Rose]]|Recipe: [[Fel Rose]] x3 ×4 + Flower of Truth → [[Bouquet of Lies]]|

|✪✪|100 Essence|150 Essence|
|✪✪✪|200 Essence|350 Essence|
|✪✪✪✪|400 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|750 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|800 Essence; 25,000 [[Coins]]|1,550 Essence; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- This weapon pairs well with the [[Baby Yeti Pet]] due to its high base [[Strength]]. It is recommended to apply the [[Reforging|Withered]] reforge to the Flower of Truth to further enhance the ability of the Baby Yeti Pet.
  - It can also provide enough [[Strength]] to maximize the [[Crit Damage]] given by the [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Helmet]] and the [[Primordial Armor|Primordial Helmet]]'s abilities, although by the time a player acquires the latter they should have enough [[Coins]] to upgrade to the [[Bouquet of Lies]], a superior weapon.
- This weapon pairs better with [[Reforging|Fabled]] due to it having more balanced [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]], which will likely result in greater damage infliction.
- Having high [[Health Regen]] can offset this weapon's [[Health]] cost, allowing for its ability to be spammed without downside.
## Trivia
- The Flower of Truth is frequently abbreviated to FoT.
- The Heat-Seeking Rose ability cannot damage the [[Sven Packmaster]] or [[Ghost|Ghosts]].
- It is possible to both left-click and right-click at the same time to deal more [[Damage]] to enemies within melee range.
- [[Sadan|Terracottas]] have an ability similar to that of the Flower of Truth, with a rose projectile that homes in on players and bounces between them.
- [[One For All]] does not buff the damage of Heat-Seeking Rose.
