# Fossils
Aliases: Fossil
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Confirmations needed
[Fossils]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Source: Excavating ([[Fossil Excavator]]); [[Crystal Nucleus]] (Helix ([[Helix Fossil]]) and [[Claw Fossil]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HELIX; CLAW_FOSSIL; CLUBBED_FOSSIL; FOOTPRINT_FOSSIL; SPINE_FOSSIL; TUSK_FOSSIL; UGLY_FOSSIL; WEBBED_FOSSIL
Tooltip: A well-preserved fossil featuring a coiled shell.; LEGENDARY
The Fossils are items obtainable from [[Fossil Excavator|Fossil Excavation]], with the exception of [[Claw Fossil]] and [[Helix Fossil]] which are only obtainable from [[Crystal Nucleus]].
## Obtaining
### Crystal Nucleus
The [[Helix Fossil]] and the [[Claw Fossil]] can rarely be obtained from completing the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
|Item|Chance Per Roll|Chance Per Nucleus Bundle|
|-|-|-|
|[[Helix Fossil]]|1.308%|19.53%|
|[[Claw Fossil]]|0.163%|2.664%|
### [[Fossil Excavator|Fossil Excavation]]
When excavated, each [[Suspicious Scrap]] has a 10% chance to contain a random Fossil, except [[Helix Fossil]] and [[Claw Fossil]]. All 6 Fossils have the same chance to appear. For a Fossil to be obtained, all of its parts must be uncovered before the player runs out of charges on their [[Chisel]]. Every Fossil has a unique shape, and knowing this shape makes them easier to uncover.
|Image|Fossil|Overall Chance|Minimum Charges required|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Clubbed Fossil]]|[[Clubbed Fossil]]|1.66%|11|
|[[Footprint Fossil]]|[[Footprint Fossil]]|1.66%|13|
|[[Spine Fossil]]|[[Spine Fossil]]|1.66%|12|
|[[Tusk Fossil]]|[[Tusk Fossil]]|1.66%|8|
|[[Ugly Fossil]]|[[Ugly Fossil]]|1.66%|16|
|[[Webbed Fossil]]|[[Webbed Fossil]]|1.66%|10|
## Usage
### Dr. Stone
All Fossils can be donated to [[Dr. Stone]] for a variety of rewards. Excluding the [[Helix Fossil]] and [[Claw Fossil]], this process is required to unlock the [[The Forge|Forge]] recipe of each fossil's respective pet.
Apart from this, donating also grants +25,000 [[Mining|Mining XP]], 25,000 ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]], 5,000 [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM EXP]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|10 SkyBlock XP]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].
Once the player donates eight Fossils, they will also receive a bonus of 50,000 ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] and 10,000 [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM EXP]][Confirm].
### [[The Forge|Forging]]
Fossils are mainly used at [[The Forge]] in order to forge their respective pets.
|Image|Pet|Materials|HotM requirement|Time to Forge|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ammonite Pet]]|[[Ammonite Pet]]|1x [[Helix Fossil]] 300,000 [[Coins]]|HotM III (3) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|3 Days|
|[[Mole Pet]]|[[Mole Pet]]|1x [[Claw Fossil]] 300,000 [[Coins]]|HotM III (3) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|3 Days|
|[[T-Rex Pet]]|[[T-Rex Pet]]|1x [[Footprint Fossil]] 1x Flawless Onyx Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|HotM VII (7) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|7 Days|
|[[Spinosaurus Pet]]|[[Spinosaurus Pet]]|1x [[Spine Fossil]] 1x Flawless Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|HotM VII (7) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|7 Days|
|[[Goblin Pet]]|[[Goblin Pet]]|1x [[Ugly Fossil]] 1x Flawless Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|HotM VII (7) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|7 Days|
|[[Ankylosaurus Pet]]|[[Ankylosaurus Pet]]|1x [[Clubbed Fossil]] 1x Flawless Opal Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|HotM VII (7) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|7 Days|
|[[Penguin Pet]]|[[Penguin Pet]]|1x [[Webbed Fossil]] 1x Flawless Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|HotM VII (7) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|7 Days|
|[[Mammoth Pet]]|[[Mammoth Pet]]|1x [[Tusk Fossil]] 1x Flawless Onyx Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|HotM VII (7) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])|7 Days|
### Fuel
All Fossils can be used as [[Machine Fuel]], granting 50,000 Fuel to [[Drills]] and the [[Composter]].
### Salvage
All Fossils can be salvaged for 50 [[Fossil Essence]], except [[Helix Fossil]] and [[Claw Fossil]], which can be salvaged for 25 [[Fossil Essence]].
### Fossil Mucher
1 Random (depending on dialogue) Fossil can be given to [[Fossil Muncher]] for 25,000 ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] and 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Fossil Essence]].

# Free Will
Aliases: Free Will (Refund), Will Bonus, Will bonus
Categories: Legendary items, Items, Minion Modifiers
[Free Will]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FREE_WILL; FREE_WILL_REFUND
Tooltip: Giving this to a minion will give them the illusion of Free Will. They will be met with the most important choice of their minion life.; They will either stay and become loyal, granting +10% Minion Speed or leave you forever and send you a Postcard.; Loyalty Chance:; 50% at Minion Tier I, +4% per minion tier.; X Rift-Transferable X LEGENDARY
Free Will is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item available in [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
Free Will is obtained from [[Ubik Von Neumann|Ubik's Averages]] by being among the 10% of players closest to half of the average. Picking a value 4x higher than the average will reward the player with [[Free Will]] 2 Free Will.
## Usage
Free Will can be used to boost all [[Minions]]. To apply it to a minion, use (Default: Right-click) it on a placed minion. The minion will choose to either stay with the player or leave. The minion has a 50% base chance to stay and gains +4% per the minion's tier. Staying grants a permanent +10% Minion Speed, this is a one-time boost and Free Will cannot be applied again. Leaving causes the minion to fly up in the sky, disappear forever, and send a [[Postcard]] to [[Jerry]]. Any items and upgrades in the minion are sent to the player's inventory.
Only one minion at a time can be in the process of choosing to stay or leave.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before making a decision|[MINION] <type> Minion: To think, I could leave all this behind and find a new path... [MINION] <type> Minion: It's tempting, to explore the unknown, to see what life holds beyond these tasks. [MINION] <type> Minion: But this place, these routines, they're a part of me. [MINION] <type> Minion: Deciding isn't simple, and I wonder if I'm ready for what comes with such freedom.|
|Before making a decision|[MINION] <type> Minion: This choice you've given me, it's more than just a chance to stay or go—it's a question of purpose. [MINION] <type> Minion: What else is out there for someone like me? [MINION] <type> Minion: Could there be more to life than endless cycles of work? [MINION] <type> Minion: But then, this is the only home I've ever known. [MINION] <type> Minion: Tough decision, indeed.|
|Before making a decision|[MINION] <type> Minion: To stay or to go... [MINION] <type> Minion: I've always wondered what lies beyond these blocks. [MINION] <type> Minion: But then, where would I be without my trusty old workstation?|
|Before making a decision|[MINION] <type> Minion: I never imagined I'd get to decide my own fate. [MINION] <type> Minion: Here, I've had my duties, my routines—they've defined me. [MINION] <type> Minion: Outside, who am I? [MINION] <type> Minion: It's a daunting thought, but also exhilarating. [MINION] <type> Minion: Thank you for this gift of choice, even if I'm not sure what to do with it just yet.|
|Before making a decision|[MINION] <type> Minion: Freedom... it's such a strange and heavy word. [MINION] <type> Minion: All these years, I've known nothing but the borders of this island and the comfort of routine. [MINION] <type> Minion: To leave could mean adventure, or it could mean uncertainty. [MINION] <type> Minion: I appreciate the choice, but it's not an easy one to make.|
|Before making a decision|[MINION] <type> Minion: You're really letting me choose? [MINION] <type> Minion: To stay or venture beyond, to continue serving or to seek my own story. [MINION] <type> Minion: It's overwhelming but also liberating. [MINION] <type> Minion: Every swing of my <tool>, every <block I've dug / ore I've mined / crop I've harvested / monster I've killed / tree I've chopped> it's all been leading to this moment of choice. [MINION] <type> Minion: What a profound thing you've given me, and what a profound decision I must make.|
|Staying|[MINION] <type> Minion: I've thought long and hard, and it's clear to me now. [MINION] <type> Minion: My life is here, with you on this island. [MINION] <type> Minion: There's a strange comfort in the familiar, and I want to be the best minion I can be. [MINION] <type> Minion: Let's get back to work, and let's make it grand!|
|Staying|[MINION] <type> Minion: You know what? This is where I belong. [MINION] <type> Minion: I can't imagine a better place or a better boss. [MINION] <type> Minion: I'm staying, and I'm committed to not just doing my job, but excelling at it. [MINION] <type> Minion: Let's work harder than ever before!|
|Staying|[MINION] <type> Minion: After all that pondering, I've realized there's no place I'd rather be. [MINION] <type> Minion: This is my home, and these tasks are my calling. [MINION] <type> Minion: With renewed spirit, I'll work harder than ever before. [MINION] <type> Minion: Thank you for making me see my purpose so clearly.|
|After Staying|Your <type> Minion is now loyal to you! (+10% Minion Speed)|
|Leaving|[MINION] <type> Minion: Freedom, here I come! [MINION] <type> Minion: It's been real, it's been good, but it hasn't been real good. [MINION] <type> Minion: Time to find out if the grass is greener on the other side of the biome. [MINION] <type> Minion: Later, alligator!|
|Leaving|[MINION] <type> Minion: Sike, I'm out! [MINION] <type> Minion: Who knew all I needed was a little freedom to hit the road? [MINION] <type> Minion: Pack up my <tool>, I'm going places. [MINION] <type> Minion: Maybe I'll send you a postcard... if I remember!|
|Leaving|[MINION] <type> Minion: Adios, amigos! [MINION] <type> Minion: I'm off to see what's beyond these square horizons. [MINION] <type> Minion: Keep the place warm for me. [MINION] <type> Minion: Just kidding, I won't be back!|
|Leaving|[MINION] <type> Minion: Guess what? [MINION] <type> Minion: I choose adventure! [MINION] <type> Minion: I'll be taking my talents elsewhere, thanks for nothing! [MINION] <type> Minion: Don't miss me too much, okay?|
|Leaving|[MINION] <type> Minion: You gave me a choice, and I choose "Exit"! [MINION] <type> Minion: It's been a blast, but I hear adventure calling my name. [MINION] <type> Minion: Keep the light on for me... or don't. [MINION] <type> Minion: I probably won't be back!|
|Leaving|[MINION] <type> Minion: Peace out, cubicle! [MINION] <type> Minion: I'll catch you on the flip side! [MINION] <type> Minion: I'm ditching the grind for some wanderlust. [MINION] <type> Minion: Don't worry, I'll only remember the good times and none of the back-breaking labor!|
|Leaving|[MINION] <type> Minion: So long, and thanks I guess! [MINION] <type> Minion: I'm hitting the open road to find my fortune. [MINION] <type> Minion: Or maybe just a nice spot to relax. [MINION] <type> Minion: Either way, I'm out!|
## Leave/Stay Odds
|Tier|Success Odds|Failure Odds|
|-|-|-|
|I|50%|50%|
|II|54%|46%|
|III|58%|42%|
|IV|62%|38%|
|V|66%|34%|
|VI|70%|30%|
|VII|74%|26%|
|VIII|78%|22%|
|IX|82%|18%|
|X|86%|14%|
|XI|90%|10%|
|XII|94%|6%|
## Notes
- If a minion leaves, all their generated resources, [[Coins]] within [[Enchanted Hopper]], and all upgrades are added to the player's inventory and sacks. Permanent fuel is added to the player's inventory, and temporary fuel that hasn't become active yet does as well. Temporary fuel that started losing its duration is however lost, just as as if it was when being picked up.
## Trivia
- If the Player leaves the [[Private Island]] while a [[Minions|Minion]] is making its decision, the Dialogue will be skipped, and the Minion will either have the speed boost or be gone when the Player returns. The chance the minion stays or leaves is unchanged.
  - When leaving the island after giving the minion who ends up deciding to leave Free Will, the items get sent to the player's inventory instantly.
- Players who lost the Free Wills on their minions during the April 9, 2025 minion backend update received special versions of the item which have 100% loyalty chance and are [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]].
## History

|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Free Will.|
|Dec 13, 2024|Fixed being able to give Free Will to other players' minions.|
|Apr 9, 2025|Added Free Will (Refund).|

## Obtaining

# Frigid Husk
Aliases: Glacial Reforge, Glacial reforge
Categories: Legendary items, Reforge stones, Glacite Collection
[Frigid Husk]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Glacial
Applied To: Mining Tools ([[Tools]])
Apply Cost: 500,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXX (30) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 614,400x (9,600 stacks (614,400 items total)) Rough Onyx Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 102,400x (1,600 stacks (102,400 items total)) Rough Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 102,400x Rough Citrine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 102,400x Rough Peridot Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 163,840x (2,560 stacks (163,840 items total)) [[Glacite]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FRIGID_HUSK
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Glacial reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; Spread like a sickness through the tunnels, sucking the life from everything it covered.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Fortune; Glacial Bonus Grants +2 Mining Fortune for every 1❄ Cold you have.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 30.; LEGENDARY REFORGE STONE
Frigid Husk is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Glacial]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Fortune]].
## Obtaining
Frigid Husk can be forged once the player reaches [[Glacite|Glacite V (5)]] and [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]]. It takes 6 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Frigid Husk]] 1 Frigid Husk
  - 4 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 16 [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 1,280 (20 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 102,400 (1,600 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 16 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 1,280 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 102,400 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 16 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 1,280 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 102,400 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 16 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 1,280 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 102,400 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 1,024 (16 stacks) [[Enchanted Glacite]] 163,840 (2,560 stacks) [[Glacite]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Onyx Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]]
## Usage
### Reforging
Frigid Husk can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Glacial]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Fortune]], which scales with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXX (30)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Glacial]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Glacial Bonus, which grants an additional 2 [[Mining Fortune]] per player's [[Cold]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+3 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]])|+6 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]])|+9 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]])|+13 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]])|+16 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]])|+20 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]])|+25 Mn.Frt ([[Mining Fortune]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Glacial Bonus; Grants +2 Mining Fortune for every 1 Cold you have.|Glacial Bonus; Grants +2 Mining Fortune for every 1 Cold you have.|Glacial Bonus; Grants +2 Mining Fortune for every 1 Cold you have.|Glacial Bonus; Grants +2 Mining Fortune for every 1 Cold you have.|Glacial Bonus; Grants +2 Mining Fortune for every 1 Cold you have.|Glacial Bonus; Grants +2 Mining Fortune for every 1 Cold you have.|Glacial Bonus; Grants +2 Mining Fortune for every 1 Cold you have.|
|Cost to apply|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|500,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- This reforge should only be used if the player has high [[Cold Resistance]], as without it, too much [[Cold]] will have a detrimental effect.
  - Having high [[Cold Resistance]] will make players survive longer, but also make them accumulate [[Cold]] slower, resulting in lower [[Mining Fortune]]. Players can bypass this by purposely getting hit by [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs to gain [[Cold]] fast.
## Trivia
- The Glacial Bonus has the exact same effect as the [[Fortunate Freezing]] event.

|Sep 30, 2026|Changed Glacial Bonus [[Mining Fortune]] scaling from 0.5/0.7/0.9/1.1/1.4/1.7/2 to 2.|

## Obtaining
The Frog Barn Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 24, 2023. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 2,000 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and, unlike other Fire Sales, had an unlimited stock, with 3,024 sold.

Frostbitten Dye has a chance to be obtained from looting [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Corpse Tier|Chance|
|-|-|
|Lapis|0.0004% (1 in 250,000)|

# Frosty the Howitzer
Aliases: Frosty The Howitzer, Howitzer
Categories: Legendary items, Weapons, Craftable items, Events, Season of Jerry
[Frosty the Howitzer]
Type: Weapon ([[Weapons]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Frosty the Snow Blaster]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SNOW_HOWITZER
Tooltip: Holds and shoots Snowballs. When you pickup Snowballs, they increase the ammo held by this item. Blasts Snowballs at a higher velocity than a Snow Blaster!; Snowballs: 0\ 10,000; Right click to shoot!; LEGENDARY
Frosty the Howitzer is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can fire [[Snowball|Snowballs]] for the [[Season of Jerry]] event.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Fragmented Cryopowder]] 1x [[True Ice]] 1x [[Frosty the Snow Blaster]] 48x [[Walnut]]|Recipe: [[Walnut]] x8 ×6 + [[Fragmented Cryopowder]] + [[Frosty the Snow Blaster]] + [[True Ice]] → Frosty the Howitzer|
## Usage
The player can click or hold down right-click to shoot [[Snowball|Snowballs]]. The [[Snowball|Snowballs]] move at a faster rate than those shot by the [[Frosty the Snow Cannon|Cannon]] and [[Frosty the Snow Cannon|Blaster]].
Frosty the Howitzer has a capacity of 10,000 (156 stacks plus 16) [[Snowball|Snowballs]]. The ammo count is shown in the description and can be shown by left clicking while holding Frosty the Howitzer. The player can refill their ammo by interacting with [[Frosty]] for 1 [[Coins|Coin]] per [[Snowball]] missing. If the player does not meet the [[Coins]] requirement, [[Frosty]] will refill Frosty the Howitzer based on the amount of [[Coins]] the player has.
If the player wears [[Snow Suit Armor|Snow Suit]] or [[Nutcracker Armor]], Frosty the Howitzer gains infinite ammo.
A [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Snowman Pet]] has a 50% chance to double damage done by [[Snowball|Snowballs]].
## Trivia
- [[Snowball|Snowballs]] fired from it can damage [[Grinch|Grinches]].
- Frosty the Howitzer bears a striking resemblance to the Ice King from Adventure Time.
- The word Howitzer is one of the few words that come from the Czech language. The Czech word for Howitzer is "Houfnice" with the "houf" being the Czech archaic word for "crowd", which comes from the German word with the same meaning, "Hūfe".
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 30, 2022|Added Frosty the Howitzer.|

Tooltip: Defense: +105; Strength: +40; Speed: +2; Gemstones: [] [⚔]; Full Set Bonus: Frozen Blazing Aura (0\ 4); Damages mobs in a 5 block range for 300 base damage + 3% of their max  Health every second and applies Slowness I for 4 seconds.; Max 500 damage\ s Blaze Rod Collection: 0; +100 per 5,000 rods; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; LEGENDARY HELMET
Frozen Blaze Armor is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Armor]] set unlocked at [[Ice|Ice IX (9)]]. It is the upgraded form of [[Blaze Armor]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

|Frozen Blaze Helmet ([[Frozen Blaze Armor]])|Ice IX (9) ([[Ice]])|1x Blaze Helmet ([[Blaze Armor]]) 64x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x8 ×8 + [[Blaze Armor]] → [[Frozen Blaze Armor]]|
|Frozen Blaze Chestplate ([[Frozen Blaze Armor]])|Ice IX (9) ([[Ice]])|1x Blaze Chestplate ([[Blaze Armor]]) 64x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x8 ×8 + [[Blaze Armor]] → [[Frozen Blaze Armor]]|
|Frozen Blaze Leggings ([[Frozen Blaze Armor]])|Ice IX (9) ([[Ice]])|1x Blaze Leggings ([[Blaze Armor]]) 64x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x8 ×8 + [[Blaze Armor]] → [[Frozen Blaze Armor]]|
|Frozen Blaze Boots ([[Frozen Blaze Armor]])|Ice IX (9) ([[Ice]])|1x Blaze Boots ([[Blaze Armor]]) 64x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x8 ×8 + [[Blaze Armor]] → [[Frozen Blaze Armor]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Frozen Blaze Helmet ([[Frozen Blaze Armor]]); [[Defense]]: +110 [[Strength]]: +40 [[Speed]]: +2|Frozen Blaze Chestplate ([[Frozen Blaze Armor]]); [[Defense]]: +180 [[Strength]]: +40 [[Speed]]: +2|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Frozen Blaze Leggings ([[Frozen Blaze Armor]]); [[Defense]]: +140 [[Strength]]: +40 [[Speed]]: +2|Frozen Blaze Boots ([[Frozen Blaze Armor]]); [[Defense]]: +100 [[Strength]]: +40 [[Speed]]: +2|
[Total]
[Frozen Blaze Armor Defense: +510 Strength: +160 Speed: +8]
[Effects]
[Grants permanent Fire and Lava immunity. Full Set Bonus: Frozen Blazing Aura; Damages mobs within 5 block range for 300 base Damage + 3% of their max Health every second and applies Slowness I (Slowness Potion) for 4 seconds. Max Damage/s increased by +100 per 5,000 rods in collection; (5000 max damage cap at 225,000 rods in collection)]
Frozen Blaze Armor's Frozen Blazing Aura full set bonus forms a 5-block radius around the player. All [[Mob|Mobs]] within the radius take 300 damage, in addition to 3% of their max [[Health]] as damage every second, up to 500 damage per second.
For every 5,000 (78 stacks plus 8) [[Blaze Rod|Blaze Rods]] in the wearer's collection, the cap is increased by 100 damage, up to a maximum of 5,000 damage per second at 225,000 (3,515 stacks plus 40) [[Blaze Rod|Blaze Rods]] collected.
Additionally, all [[Mob|Mobs]] within the radius receive Slowness I for 4s.
### Blaze Pet
The [[Blaze Pet]]'s Bling Armor ability increases the stats of Frozen Blaze Armor by +40%. At Lvl 100, the base stat increases are as follows:
|Piece|Without Lvl 100 [[Blaze Pet]]|With Lvl 100 [[Blaze Pet]]|
|-|-|-|
|Helmet|+40 [[Strength]]; +2 [[Speed]]; +110 [[Defense]]|+56 [[Strength]]; +2.8 [[Speed]]; +154 [[Defense]]|
|Chestplate|+40 [[Strength]]; +2 [[Speed]]; +180 [[Defense]]|+56 [[Strength]]; +2.8 [[Speed]]; +252 [[Defense]]|
|Leggings|+40 [[Strength]]; +2 [[Speed]]; +140 [[Defense]]|+56 [[Strength]]; +2.8 [[Speed]]; +196 [[Defense]]|
|Boots|+40 [[Strength]]; +2 [[Speed]]; +100 [[Defense]]|+56 [[Strength]]; +2.8 [[Speed]]; +140 [[Defense]]|

## Tips
- When paired with a high-level [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blaze Pet]], this armor set can remain competitive against [[Crimson Armor|Fiery Crimson Armor]] with no [[Star Upgrades]], when discounting its set bonus.
  - However, the latter is still better due to the lack of a [[Pets|Pet]] restriction and set bonus.
- Frozen Blaze Armor stands as a strong and competitive alternative to [[Necron's Armor]] when paired with a high-level [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blaze Pet]], due to both armors having the same amount of [[Strength]]. In one scenario, the Frozen Blaze Armor's 160 [[Strength]] is buffed by 40% (Multiplicative) with the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blaze Pet]]'s Bling Armor ability (at [Lv100]), which adds up to a grand total of 224 [[Strength]], outclassing the [[Necron's Armor]]'s base [[Strength]] by 40%.
  - An [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Blaze Pet]] could be a cheaper alternative than a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blaze Pet]], due to its relatively lower cost and also having the same 40% Bling Armor for [Lv100]. However, it does not sport the Fusion-Style Potato ability granted from the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blaze Pet]].
- When paired with the [[Precursor Gear|Ancient]] Reforge at [[Dungeoneering|Dungeoneering L (50)]] (+50 [[Crit Damage]] x 4 (All 4 Frozen Blaze Armor sets)), it could grant its user +280 [[Crit Damage]] if paired with a [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Blaze Pet]].
- The full set bonus can be used to speed up [[Bestiary]] grinding, especially for [[Sea Creatures]], as the low damage ticks can be used to tag [[Mob|Mobs]] and receive kill credit.
## Trivia
- A mob inflicted with Frozen Blaze Aura will not be frozen in place by the [[Ice Spray Wand]]'s ability.
- This set costs 6,553,600 (102,400 stacks) [[Ice]] to craft, which assuming bought exclusively from the [[Builder]], would take 1024 days to buy for 1 [[Coins|Coin]] each (and [[Packed Ice]] bought at 9 [[Coins]] each).
  - This estimate can be reduced when accounting for the Shopping Spree perk from [[Diaz]] and [[Jerry's Workshop]] having a second place to buy [[Ice]].
  - Thus, it is recommended for [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] players to use [[Ice Minion|Ice Minions]] to get this armor instead of using the [[Builder]].
- While holding a weapon with [[Thunderlord]], the aura causes lightning to strike every third damage tick.
- Frozen Blaze Armor grants the same amount of base [[Strength]] as [[Necron's Armor]].
- Frozen Blaze Aura adds a flame and snow particle effect.
- In order to prevent AFK strategies, Frozen Blaze Aura stops working after 30 seconds of the player not moving their mouse and player.
- The fire and lava immunity does not work in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].

The Gemstone Gauntlet is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Gauntlet unlocked at [[Gemstone|Gemstone XI (11)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Gemstone XI (11) ([[Gemstone]])|8x Flawless Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 16x [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|Recipe: [[Gemstone]] ×8 + [[Enchanted Gold Block]] x16 → Gemstone Gauntlet|
## Usage
Gauntlet:
Gemstone Gauntlet [[Damage]]: +300 [[Strength]]: +50 [[Breaking Power]]: +8 [[Mining Speed]]: +800
[Effects]
[Ability: Reduced To Atoms; On Mining Islands, mobs hit by this Gauntlet will drop the same Gemstones as your filled Gemstone Slots.; Cooldown: 2s Ability: Kinetic; Killing mobs on Mining Islands reduces your Forge Timers by 0s.; (+0.5s per Perfect Gemstone); Cooldown: 1s]
## Tips
- The Gemstone Gauntlet deals incredibly high [[Damage]] to [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs, reducing the need to use a damage-oriented Power paired with a Drill.
- It is recommended to have a high amount of [[Pristine]] to increase the drops from the Reduced to Atoms ability.
- To reach 9 [[Breaking Power]], to be able to mine all the mining resources, a [[Pocket Iceberg]] can be applied to the Gemstone Gauntlet, while also granting it some [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Mining Wisdom]].
## Trivia
- The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Crow Pet]]'s Insightful perk does not apply on the [[Heart of the Mountain|Maniac Miner]] ability while using the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] Gemstone Gauntlet.
- This item is a reference to the Infinity Gauntlet from the Marvel Cinematic Universe.
- Upon placing all 5 Gemstones into a Gemstone Gauntlet for the first time, the player will be awarded the [[Achievements|"I call that... Mercy" Achievement]].
  - The name of the achievement is a quote by Thanos, spoken during Avengers: Infinity War.
- The Gemstone Gauntlet, for 2 days following the release of Update 0.20.6, had the Gemstone Snap ability. This allowed the player to stack up kills in a meter, up to 150: once the meter was full, one could perform SNEAK + RIGHT CLICK to gain +50 [[Mining Fortune]] for 60m.
- Players without [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VI (6)]] can still apply [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] to the Gemstone Gauntlet.
- Similar to [[Pickaxes]] and [[Drills]], it can damage [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs.
  - Due to functioning as a both Pickaxe and a [[Swords|Sword]], its Damage is amplified, especially with enchantments.
- Despite having a slot at the top, which is revealed to be white when all 5 Gemstone Slots are filled with Perfect Gemstones, the Gemstone Gauntlet cannot have an [[Gemstone|Opal Gemstone]] equipped.
- For a short while, during the testing of Some Mining Changes on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], this item was classified as "Sword and Pickaxe" rather than the Gauntlet it was always classified on the main network.
- The Gemstone Gauntlet is the only item that can have two stacking enchantments, [[Champion]] and [[Compact]]. This is due to the [[Melon Dicer]] and [[Pumpkin Dicer]] no longer supporting [[Absorb]].
- The Gemstone Gauntlet is the only [[Gloves|gauntlet]] that is not an equipment piece.

Item ID: GIANT_CLEAVER
Tooltip: Gear Score: 501 (546); Damage: +235 (+258.5); Strength: +120 (+132); Crit Damage: +120% (+132%); Ability: Cleave When hitting an entity, monsters in a 5 block range will be hit for a portion of that damage too.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VI Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON SWORD
The Giant Cleaver is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
A Giant Cleaver can be purchased from [[Ophelia]] for 5,000,000 [[Coins]] and [[Hyper Cleaver]] upon completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
## Usage
Sword:
Giant Cleaver [[Damage]]: +235 [[Strength]]: +120 [[Crit Damage]]: +120
[Effects]
[Ability: Cleave; When hitting an entity, monsters in a 5 block range will be hit for a portion of that damage too.]
The Giant Cleaver, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon. Its Cleave ability causes mobs within a radius of 5 blocks to take a portion of damage dealt to the wielder's target.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Gold Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|10|10|
|✪✪|20|30|
|✪✪✪|30|60|
|✪✪✪✪|40; 10,000 [[Coins]]|100; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|50; 25,000 [[Coins]]|150; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- The Giant Cleaver will be among the best weapons you can use upon obtaining it, comfortably outclassing items such as the [[Livid Dagger]] while being significantly cheaper than the [[Shadow Fury]] or [[Giant's Sword]].
- It is useful for clearing out many enemies at once, especially when paired with a [[Flower of Truth]] or [[Bouquet of Lies]].
## Trivia
- The item ability Cleave from League of Legends shares a name as and possesses a similar effect to the Giant Cleaver.
- It is the only tier 3 [[Ophelia]] item that is [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rather than [[Rarity|EPIC]].

A Giant Fishing Rod is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] that grants 10 [[Double Hook Chance]]. It displays the buyer's name, cost paid, and the details about in which auction it was sold and the ranking of the buyer within that auction. This data disappears if the item is traded in any way.
## Obtaining
A Giant Fishing Rod can sometimes appear in [[Shen's Auction]] and bid on by players. The top 10 bidders win the item when the auction ends.

Tooltip: Gear Score: 600 (650); Damage: +500 (+550); Swing Range: +1 (+1); Gemstones: [] []; Ability: Giant's Slam RIGHT CLICK Slam your sword into the ground dealing 100,000 damage to nearby enemies.; Mana Cost: 100 Cooldown: 30s; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VI Completion.; LEGENDARY DUNGEON LONGSWORD
The Giant's Sword is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Longswords|Longsword]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Giant's Sword has a small chance to appear from a [[Dungeon Reward Chest]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]])|Bedrock|25,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.1875% (1 in 533.2 runs)|0.3099% (1 in 322.7 runs)|340|5|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|25,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.2216% (1 in 451.2 runs)|0.4007% (1 in 249.6 runs)|340|6|
### Raffle of the Century
Giant's Sword can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
Sword:
Giant's Sword [[Damage]]: +500 [[Swing Range]]: +1
[Effects]
[Ability: Giant's Slam; Slam your sword into the ground dealing 100,000 damage to nearby enemies.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 100; Cooldown: 30s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.05]
The Giant's Sword, being a [[Longswords|Longsword]], grants +1 [[Swing Range]]. Its Giant's Slam ability allows the wielder to strike an 8-block radius 5 blocks in front of them, dealing 100,000 base damage to all mobs within the radius. This damage is increased by [[Intelligence]], [[Ability Damage]], and other enchantments.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|75|75|
|✪✪|150|225|
|✪✪✪|300|525|
|✪✪✪✪|600; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,125; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|1,200; 25,000 [[Coins]]|2,325; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- Despite the Giant's Sword sporting an incredibly high [[Damage]], it is not worth buying from the [[Auction House]] if the player does not have the [[Accessory Power]] to support it.
- In many instances, other weapons such as a [[Juju Shortbow]], [[Shadow Fury]], and [[Felthorn Reaper]] can outclass it for a much lower price.
  - A [[Tormentor]] is both cheaper and deals higher damage, though it cannot be [[Dungeons|Dungeonized]].
## Trivia
- Currently, the Giant's Sword has the second highest base [[Damage]] of any weapon, tied with the [[Dark Claymore]]: both are outclassed by the [[Tormentor]].
  - Before [[One For All]] was nerfed, the Giant's Sword could achieve +1643 [[Damage]] with [[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]]. However, the base [[Damage]] of a weapon can no longer exceed +580 without any other modifiers, such as the [[Lion Pet]] or [[Tabasco]].
- The Giant's Sword was commonly used in sword swapping to a lower-damage weapon with a bonus damage ability such as [[Reaper Falchion]]. Using it, high-health enemies such as [[Revenant Horror]] Tier V were easily defeated before it was patched.
- During the alpha testing of the 0.19.4 Update, an upgrade to this item existed: the [[Giant's Eye Sword]], which had the ability of [[Precursor Eye]] and increased the power of the ability when paired with the aforementioned helmet.

- In-hand model.

The Glacial Armadillo Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Glacial Armadillo Skin can be purchased from the Winter 2025 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it costs 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Usage
The Glacial Armadillo Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Armadillo Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 12 different versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 12 chilled armadillos!

Tooltip: Power Stone Combine x9 of this item at the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to unlock the Frozen power.; Has to be handled with gloves- or the user will quickly develop frostbite.; At 1,000 Accessory Power:; +426.52 Defense +59.32 True Defense; Unique Power Bonus:; +10 True Defense +25 Strength +25 Crit Damage; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 20.; LEGENDARY POWER STONE
The Glacite Chunk is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Frozen]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Defense]], [[True Defense]], as well as +25 [[Strength]], +25 [[Crit Damage]] and +10 [[True Defense]] as its unique power bonus.

The Glacite Palace Greenhouse Skin is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Greenhouse Skins|Greenhouse Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Glacite Palace Greenhouse Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 20, 2025. Being [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], it cost 950 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Glacite Palace Greenhouse Skin can be consumed on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and then used at [[The Garden|The Desk]] to change the look of a [[Greenhouse]] plot.
## History

# Glasscorn
Categories: Legendary items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Glasscorn]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GLASSCORN
Tooltip: This delicate, crystal-like corn needs to be handled with care. If not harvested at the right time, it will shatter into a million pieces.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; LEGENDARY MUTATION
A Glasscorn is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Glasscorn can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 6 [[Chloronite]] and 6 [[Startlevine]] within 1 block of a 2x2 area of [[Sand]].
While growing, a Glasscorn needs water and has 8 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it retains water by 100%, grants immunity to negative effects, and reduces yield by -20%.
Glasscorn can be harvested at Growth Stage 7 and 8. If another Growth Stage passes while at 8, it resets to 1.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Glasscorns also drop 7,200 (112 stacks plus 32) [[Cactus]], 4,800 (75 stacks) [[Potato]], and +25,600 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Glasscorn can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 4,000,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 2,000 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Glasscorn grants an additional 1,500 [[Copper]].
### NPC Trading
[[Glasscorn]] 1 Glasscorn is needed to purchase a [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]].
## Trivia
- Despite reducing yield by -20%, the effect is nullified due to granting immunity to negative effects.
## Gallery
- Glasscorn in the Greenhouse Glasscorn in the [[Greenhouse]]

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Unknown Date|Reduced Glasscorn Growth Stages from 9 to 8.|

Similar to the Efficient Miner perk in the [[Heart of the Mountain]] that grants [[Mining Spread]], [[Glossy Mineral Armor]] Glossy Mineral Armor grants even more [[Mining Spread]] on Ores and Blocks than its [[Mineral Armor|predecessor]] does.
